<p id="mwQA">The <b id="mwQQ">2022 Bratislava shooting</b> was an attack that occurred on 12 October 2022 outside Tepláreň, a gay bar in Bratislava, Slovakia.<sup about="#mwt61" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BBC_Oct13_12-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BBC_Oct13"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-BBC_Oct13-12"}}'><a href="#cite_note-BBC_Oct13-12" id="mwQg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwQw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRA">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRQ">]</span></span></a></sup> A 19-year-old perpetrator shot and killed two people and wounded a third outside the venue's entrance.<sup about="#mwt64" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Reuters_Initial_13-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Reuters_Initial"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Reuters_Initial-13"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Reuters_Initial-13" id="mwRg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwRw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSA">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSQ">]</span></span></a></sup> Following an investigation by the National Crime Agency (NAKA), Slovak authorities reclassified the incident from a hate-motivated murder to an act of domestic terrorism, designating it as the first terrorist attack in modern Slovak history.<sup about="#mwt67" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Sme_Terrorism_14-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Sme_Terrorism"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Sme_Terrorism-14"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Sme_Terrorism-14" id="mwSg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwSw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTA">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt70" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Boell_Terror_15-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Boell_Terror"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Boell_Terror-15"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Boell_Terror-15" id="mwTg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwTw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUA">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUQ">]</span></span></a></sup> a well-known spot frequented by the local <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LGBTQ" title="LGBTQ" class="mw-redirect" id="mwUg">LGBTQ</a> community.<sup about="#mwt73" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Sme_16-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Sme"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Sme-16"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Sme-16" id="mwUw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwVA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVQ">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt76" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-N_6-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"N"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-N-6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-N-6" id="mwVw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwWA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWQ">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWg">]</span></span></a></sup> The shooting claimed two victims: Juraj Vankulič, a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Non-binary_gender" title="Non-binary gender" class="mw-redirect" id="mwWw">non-binary</a> person, and Matúš Horváth, a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bisexuality" title="Bisexuality" id="mwXA">bisexual</a> man.<sup about="#mwt79" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-17" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-17"}}'><a href="#cite_note-17" id="mwXQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwXg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXw">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt82" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Brezáni_1-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Brezáni"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Brezáni-1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Brezáni-1" id="mwYQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwYg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYw">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwZA">]</span></span></a></sup> The perpetrator was found dead from a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide_by_gunshot" title="Suicide by gunshot" class="mw-redirect" id="mwZQ">self-inflicted gunshot</a> the morning after the attack.<sup about="#mwt85" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-18" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-18"}}'><a href="#cite_note-18" id="mwZg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwZw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwaA">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwaQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwag">The investigation classified the shooting as a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terrorism" title="Terrorism" id="mwaw">terrorist attack</a> and found that primary targets included high level politicians.<sup about="#mwt88" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-19" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-19"}}'><a href="#cite_note-19" id="mwbA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwbQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbg">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt91" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-20" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-20"}}'><a href="#cite_note-20" id="mwcA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwcQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwcg">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwcw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt92" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Brezáni_1-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Brezáni"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Brezáni-1" id="mwdA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwdQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwdg">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwdw">]</span></span></a></sup>
According to the shooter's own words on a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/4chan" title="4chan" id="mweA">4chan</a> thread that he authored, "2-1 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glossary_of_video_game_terms#Kill-death_ratio" title="Glossary of video game terms" id="mweQ">kdr</a>, not my problem. wish i couldve gone higher but whatever. wanted to bag the prime minister but i didn't get lucky with his car arriving".<sup about="#mwt95" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-RSIS_Bratislava_21-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"RSIS_Bratislava"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-RSIS_Bratislava-21"}}'><a href="#cite_note-RSIS_Bratislava-21" id="mweg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwew"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwfA">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwfQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt99" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-22" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-22"}}'><a href="#cite_note-22" id="mwfg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwfw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwgA">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwgQ">]</span></span></a></sup> The primary target was <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eduard_Heger" title="Eduard Heger" id="mwgg">Eduard Heger</a>; the attack on Tepláreň was a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Contingency_plan" title="Contingency plan" id="mwgw">plan B</a>.<sup about="#mwt102" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-23" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-23"}}'><a href="#cite_note-23" id="mwhA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwhQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhg">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" id="mwiA" data-mw='{"autoGenerated":true}'/></section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwiQ" aria-labelledby="Shooting"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Shooting">Shooting</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=2022_Bratislava_shooting&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Shooting"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwig"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Zámocká_new_street_2.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwiw"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Zámocká_new_street_2.jpg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/90/Z%C3%A1mock%C3%A1_new_street_2.jpg/250px-Z%C3%A1mock%C3%A1_new_street_2.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/90/Z%C3%A1mock%C3%A1_new_street_2.jpg/500px-Z%C3%A1mock%C3%A1_new_street_2.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="2560" data-file-height="1920" data-file-type="bitmap" height="188" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwjA"/></a><figcaption id="mwjQ">The building where Tepláreň is located. Before the shooting, the killer hid in the alcove on the right of the picture</figcaption></figure>

<p id="mwjg">According to security camera footage, the attacker, dressed in all black, wearing a black cap and a black face mask, arrived at the crime scene at approximately 18:35 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_European_Summer_Time" title="Central European Summer Time" id="mwjw">CEST</a>. He then hid in an alcove next door from Tepláreň for about half an hour.<sup about="#mwt105" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-camera_24-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"camera"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-camera-24"}}'><a href="#cite_note-camera-24" id="mwkA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwkQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwkg">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwkw">]</span></span></a></sup> At 19:11, the shooter fired eight shots, killing two and injuring one. A minute later, the first report was received at the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/112_(emergency_telephone_number)" title="112 (emergency telephone number)" id="mwlA">112 emergency line</a>.<sup about="#mwt106" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Brezáni_1-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Brezáni"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Brezáni-1" id="mwlQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwlg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlw">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwmA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwmQ">First responders arrived at the scene at 19:16.<sup about="#mwt107" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Brezáni_1-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Brezáni"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Brezáni-1" id="mwmg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwmw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwnA">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwnQ">]</span></span></a></sup> They reported two people dead, and a third victim was transported to a hospital in stable condition. The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Slovak_Police_Force" title="Slovak Police Force" class="mw-redirect" id="mwng">police</a> closed off all surrounding streets and began searching for the killer with the aid of a police helicopter.<sup about="#mwt110" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-25" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-25"}}'><a href="#cite_note-25" id="mwnw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwoA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwoQ">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwog">]</span></span></a></sup> At 19:48, police had reported the shooting on social media and warned the public to stay vigilant, as the killer remained at large.<sup about="#mwt111" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-N_6-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"N"}}'><a href="#cite_note-N-6" id="mwow"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwpA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpQ">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpg">]</span></span></a></sup> Sometime between 8pm and 10pm, the shooter returned and spent time at home, informed his parents of the act, before departing once more. The parents did not report him to the Slovak authorities.<sup about="#mwt112" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Brezáni_1-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Brezáni"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Brezáni-1" id="mwpw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwqA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwqQ">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwqg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwqw">By 22:30, the police had identified the perpetrator as Juraj Krajčík with the aid of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Slovak_Information_Service" title="Slovak Information Service" class="mw-redirect" id="mwrA">SIS</a>. At 22:57, Krajčík messaged his father that they "will see each other on the other side". On 13 October 2022, at 5:15, the police received the location of the shooter's phone, provided by the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mobile_network_operator" title="Mobile network operator" id="mwrQ">operator</a>. At around 7am, the attacker was found dead from a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide" title="Suicide" id="mwrg">self-inflicted</a> gunshot wound in a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bratislava" title="Bratislava" id="mwrw">Bratislava</a> park.<sup about="#mwt113" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Brezáni_1-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Brezáni"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Brezáni-1" id="mwsA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwsQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwsg">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwsw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt116" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-26" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-26"}}'><a href="#cite_note-26" id="mwtA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwtQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwtg">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwtw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt119" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-27" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-27"}}'><a href="#cite_note-27" id="mwuA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwuQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwug">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwuw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mwvA" aria-labelledby="Investigation"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Investigation">Investigation</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=2022_Bratislava_shooting&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Investigation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwvQ">Police did not initially determine a motive for the shooting.<sup about="#mwt123" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-28" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-28"}}'><a href="#cite_note-28" id="mwvg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwvw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwwA">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwwQ">]</span></span></a></sup> On 14 October, the police confirmed it as being investigated as a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hate_crime" title="Hate crime" id="mwwg">hate crime</a> with the possibility for the crime to be reclassified as a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terrorism" title="Terrorism" id="mwww">terrorist attack</a>.<sup about="#mwt126" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:0_29-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":0"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:0-29"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:0-29" id="mwxA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwxQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwxg">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwxw">]</span></span></a></sup> The same day, the police stated they did not have any information about the attacker's sympathies for extreme ideologies prior to the killing. The police refused to confirm or deny whether the attacker had foreign accomplices, but confirmed that the investigation of the crime will include <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Interpol" title="Interpol" id="mwyA">Interpol</a> and the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Federal_Bureau_of_Investigation" title="Federal Bureau of Investigation" id="mwyQ">FBI</a>.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt120" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwyg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Citation needed","href":"./Template:Citation_needed"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"April 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_April_2025" about="#mwt120"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt120" id="mwyw"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (April 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

<p id="mwzA">The police confirmed reports that the attacker returned to his home after the shooting. He had a fight with his parents who reportedly knew of the attack but did not report it to the police. The attacker switched the murder weapon with a different one, which he later used for suicide. The murder weapon was found in the house alongside a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide_note" title="Suicide note" id="mwzQ">suicide note</a>. The parents were under investigation for failure to report the suspicious behavior of their son to the police.<sup about="#mwt129" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-30" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-30"}}'><a href="#cite_note-30" id="mwzg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwzw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw0A">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw0Q">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt132" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-31" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-31"}}'><a href="#cite_note-31" id="mw0g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw0w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1A">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1Q">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt135" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-32" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-32"}}'><a href="#cite_note-32" id="mw1g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw1w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw2A">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw2Q">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mw2g">The investigation was closed in December 2023, confirming Juraj Krajčík was the perpetrator of the act. According to the special prosecutor, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_Lipšic" title="Daniel Lipšic" id="mw2w">Daniel Lipšic</a>, Krajčík initially targeted the Prime Minister <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eduard_Heger" title="Eduard Heger" id="mw3A">Eduard Heger</a> and waited in front of his house but changed his target to Tepláreň when he realized he would not be able to get close to the politician. Krajčík called a suicide prevention line two days before the deed, saying he was "very afraid but had to die". It was further confirmed that Krajčík's parents were aware of their son's actions and his plans to commit suicide but took no action to obstruct the plans or inform the authorities.<sup about="#mwt138" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-33" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-33"}}'><a href="#cite_note-33" id="mw3Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw3g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw3w">[</span>32<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw4A">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mw4Q" aria-labelledby="Victims"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Victims">Victims</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=2022_Bratislava_shooting&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Victims"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mw4g">The shooting claimed two victims: Juraj Vankulič, a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Non-binary_gender" title="Non-binary gender" class="mw-redirect" id="mw4w">non-binary</a> person, and Matúš Horváth, a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bisexuality" title="Bisexuality" id="mw5A">bisexual</a> man.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="4" id="mw5Q" aria-labelledby="Juraj_Vankulič"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Juraj_Vankulič"><span id="Juraj_Vankuli.C4.8D" typeof="mw:FallbackId"></span>Juraj Vankulič</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=2022_Bratislava_shooting&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: Juraj Vankulič"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mw5g">Juraj Vankulič was born in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Žilina" title="Žilina" id="mw5w">Žilina</a> on 1 February 1996, to their mother Dana, not having met their biological father. As a child they were <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baptised" title="Baptised" class="mw-redirect" id="mw6A">baptised</a>, and used to sing at a local church. They had a close relationship with their step-father and their younger brother Samko.<sup about="#mwt141" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-34" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-34"}}'><a href="#cite_note-34" id="mw6Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw6g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw6w">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw7A">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mw7Q">Growing up, Vankulič went to study abroad in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Denmark" title="Denmark" id="mw7g">Denmark</a>. They held a C2 level <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cambridge" title="Cambridge" id="mw7w">Cambridge</a> English language proficiency certificate. After returning from Denmark, they were employed at a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/H&amp;M" title="H&amp;M" id="mw8A">H&amp;M</a> clothing store in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bratislava" title="Bratislava" id="mw8Q">Bratislava</a>,<sup about="#mwt144" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-35" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-35"}}'><a href="#cite_note-35" id="mw8g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw8w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw9A">[</span>34<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw9Q">]</span></span></a></sup> with Vankulič working as a visual merchandiser.<sup about="#mwt147" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-36" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-36"}}'><a href="#cite_note-36" id="mw9g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw9w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw-A">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw-Q">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mw-g">Around the age of 15, they <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coming_out" title="Coming out" id="mw-w">came out</a> as gay and later as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Non-binary_gender" title="Non-binary gender" class="mw-redirect" id="mw_A">nonbinary</a>, using he/they pronouns. Among their interests and hobbies were poetry, dance, fashion and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drag_queen" title="Drag queen" id="mw_Q">drag performance</a> under the name Sue Cidal. Their dream was to move to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prague" title="Prague" id="mw_g">Prague</a>.<sup about="#mwt150" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-37" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-37"}}'><a href="#cite_note-37" id="mw_w"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAQA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQE">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQI">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="5" id="mwAQM" aria-labelledby="Perpetrator"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Perpetrator">Perpetrator</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=2022_Bratislava_shooting&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Perpetrator"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAQQ">The perpetrator was identified as 19-year old <b id="mwAQU">Juraj Krajčik</b> (28 July 2003 — 13 October 2022). The day prior to the attack, Twitter account <i id="mwAQY">NTMA0315</i> owned by Krajčík, published statements including "I have made my decision", "it will be done" and "Race First. Always". It also posted links to an original <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Manifesto" title="Manifesto" id="mwAQc">manifesto</a> with extremist far-right <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antisemitism" title="Antisemitism" id="mwAQg">anti-semitic</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Homophobia" title="Homophobia" id="mwAQk">homophobic</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Transphobia" title="Transphobia" id="mwAQo">transphobic</a> views. In addition to Twitter, the murderer also posted his pictures on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/4chan" title="4chan" id="mwAQs">4chan</a>. His Twitter account had been suspended by the time the perpetrator's body was discovered.<sup about="#mwt151" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-N_6-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"N"}}'><a href="#cite_note-N-6" id="mwAQw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAQ0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQ4">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQ8">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwARA">The Twitter account also contained several <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Selfie" title="Selfie" id="mwARE">selfies</a> of himself, including one from August 2022 in front of Tepláreň and another one in front of the home of the Prime Minister <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eduard_Heger" title="Eduard Heger" id="mwARI">Eduard Heger</a>, whom he also mentioned as the top priority target in his manifesto.<sup about="#mwt154" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-38" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-38"}}'><a href="#cite_note-38" id="mwARM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwARQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARU">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARY">]</span></span></a></sup> In addition to Heger, the manifesto mentioned other politicians as targets, including former Minister of Education <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Branislav_Gröhling" title="Branislav Gröhling" id="mwARc">Branislav Gröhling</a> and opposition leader and former Prime Minister <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_Fico" title="Robert Fico" id="mwARg">Robert Fico</a>.<sup about="#mwt157" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-39" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-39"}}'><a href="#cite_note-39" id="mwARk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwARo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARs">[</span>38<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAR0">According to Matej Medvecký, a counter-extremism expert, the murderer had also been active in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terrorgram" title="Terrorgram" id="mwAR4">Terrorgram</a> communities and subscribed to the ideology of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Militant_accelerationism" title="Militant accelerationism" id="mwAR8">militant accelerationism</a>, supported by terrorist groups such as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Atomwaffen_Division" title="Atomwaffen Division" id="mwASA">Atomwaffen Division</a>.<sup about="#mwt158" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-komentare.sme.sk_5-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"komentare.sme.sk"}}'><a href="#cite_note-komentare.sme.sk-5" id="mwASE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwASI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASM">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASQ">]</span></span></a></sup> On Terrorgram, which Krajčík cited as inspiration for the shooting, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dallas_Humber" title="Dallas Humber" id="mwASU">Dallas Humber</a> called him "St. Juraj Krajčík, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brenton_Tarrant" title="Brenton Tarrant" class="mw-redirect" id="mwASY">Tarrant's</a> sixth disciple and Terrorgram's first saint", an instance of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terrorgram#Sanctification" title="Terrorgram" id="mwASc">sanctification done in the group</a>.<sup about="#mwt159" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:1_40-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:1-40" id="mwASg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwASk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASo">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASs">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt162" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-41" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-41"}}'><a href="#cite_note-41" id="mwASw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAS0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAS4">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAS8">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<section data-mw-section-id="6" id="mwATA" aria-labelledby="Manifesto"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Manifesto">Manifesto</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=2022_Bratislava_shooting&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Manifesto"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwATE">Just a few hours before the attack, links to a 65-page long manifesto were posted on Twitter. In the document, the author does not provide their name, claiming it is not of importance and "will be known later anyway", but identifies himself as a man of Slovak origin born on 28 July 2003, who has decided to "execute an operation" against "the enemies of the white race".</p>

<p id="mwATI">The manifesto <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antisemitism" title="Antisemitism" id="mwATM">blames Jews</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Violence_against_LGBT_people" title="Violence against LGBT people" class="mw-redirect" id="mwATQ">LGBT people</a> for "causing harm to white people" and celebrates far-right murderers, including <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anders_Behring_Breivik" title="Anders Behring Breivik" id="mwATU">Anders Breivik</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brenton_Tarrant" title="Brenton Tarrant" class="mw-redirect" id="mwATY">Brenton Tarrant</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Poway_synagogue_shooting" title="Poway synagogue shooting" class="mw-redirect" id="mwATc">John Earnest</a>.<sup about="#mwt163" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-N_6-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"N"}}'><a href="#cite_note-N-6" id="mwATg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwATk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATo">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATs">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt164" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Sme_16-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Sme"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Sme-16" id="mwATw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAT0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAT4">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAT8">]</span></span></a></sup>  The manifesto mentions the author's hatred for Islam as his first politically incorrect position.<sup about="#mwt167" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-42" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-42"}}'><a href="#cite_note-42" id="mwAUA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAUE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUI">[</span>41<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUM">]</span></span></a></sup> The text also attributes <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/COVID-19_pandemic" title="COVID-19 pandemic" id="mwAUQ">COVID-19 pandemic</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vaccination" title="Vaccination" id="mwAUU">vaccination</a> measures to a "Jewish plot to train the white race to be obedient" and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Holocaust_denial" title="Holocaust denial" id="mwAUY">denies the Holocaust</a>.<sup about="#mwt170" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-43" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-43"}}'><a href="#cite_note-43" id="mwAUc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAUg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUk">[</span>42<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUo">]</span></span></a></sup> It also glorifies a 22-year-old Slovak man in particular, known on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Telegram_(software)" title="Telegram (software)" id="mwAUs">Telegram</a> as <i id="mwAUw">Slovakbro</i>, who was arrested by the police in May 2022 for promoting right-wing extremism and calling for the overthrow of the democratic system in Slovakia by means of sabotage and terrorism.</p>

<p id="mwAU0">The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Telegram_(software)" title="Telegram (software)" id="mwAU4">Telegram</a> group known as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terrorgram" title="Terrorgram" id="mwAU8">Terrorgram</a> was mentioned in his manifesto, and credited with "Building the future of the White revolution, one publication at a time."<sup about="#mwt173" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:1_40-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":1"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:1-40"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:1-40" id="mwAVA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAVE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVI">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="7" id="mwAVQ" aria-labelledby="Aftermath"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Aftermath">Aftermath</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=2022_Bratislava_shooting&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Aftermath"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAVU">On 13 October 2022, president <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zuzana_Čaputová" title="Zuzana Čaputová" id="mwAVY">Zuzana Čaputová</a> visited Tepláreň to commemorate the victims.<sup about="#mwt176" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-44" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-44"}}'><a href="#cite_note-44" id="mwAVc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAVg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVk">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVo">]</span></span></a></sup> She gave a short speech to the media. An improvised march to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SNP_Square_(Bratislava)" title="SNP Square (Bratislava)" id="mwAVs">SNP square</a> followed, with hundreds of attendees.<sup about="#mwt179" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-45" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-45"}}'><a href="#cite_note-45" id="mwAVw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAV0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAV4">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAV8">]</span></span></a></sup> Another march took place the same day in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Košice" title="Košice" id="mwAWA">Košice</a>, with a few hundred participants.<sup about="#mwt182" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-46" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-46"}}'><a href="#cite_note-46" id="mwAWE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAWI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWM">[</span>45<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAWU">On 14 October 2022, a vigil titled "March against Hatred", organized by the local LGBT advocacy group <i id="mwAWY">Iniciatíva Inakost,</i> took place in Bratislava. Participants marched from Tepláreň to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SNP_Square_(Bratislava)" title="SNP Square (Bratislava)" id="mwAWc">SNP square</a>, where the proprietor of Tepláreň, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/President_of_Slovakia" title="President of Slovakia" id="mwAWg">president</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zuzana_Čaputová" title="Zuzana Čaputová" id="mwAWk">Zuzana Čaputová</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mayor_of_Bratislava" title="Mayor of Bratislava" id="mwAWo">mayor of Bratislava</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matúš_Vallo" title="Matúš Vallo" id="mwAWs">Matúš Vallo</a> and governor of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bratislava_Region" title="Bratislava Region" id="mwAWw">Bratislava region</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Juraj_Droba" title="Juraj Droba" id="mwAW0">Juraj Droba</a> as well as other Slovak politicians and celebrities gave speeches commemorating the victims, denouncing the crime and expressing support to the LGBT community. <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prime_Minister_of_Slovakia" title="Prime Minister of Slovakia" id="mwAW4">Prime Minister</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eduard_Heger" title="Eduard Heger" id="mwAW8">Eduard Heger</a> was in the crowd, but did not give a speech. It is estimated that around 15,000 to 20,000 people have participated.<sup about="#mwt185" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-47" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-47"}}'><a href="#cite_note-47" id="mwAXA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAXE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXI">[</span>46<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXM">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt188" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-48" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-48"}}'><a href="#cite_note-48" id="mwAXQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAXU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXY">[</span>47<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXc">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt191" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-49" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-49"}}'><a href="#cite_note-49" id="mwAXg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAXk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXo">[</span>48<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXs">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAXw">On 16 October 2022, a protest took place in front of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Council_(Slovakia)" title="National Council (Slovakia)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAX0">Slovak National Council</a> building as a reaction to the attack.<sup about="#mwt194" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-50" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-50"}}'><a href="#cite_note-50" id="mwAX4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAX8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYA">[</span>49<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYE">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="8" id="mwAYI" aria-labelledby="Reactions"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Reactions">Reactions</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=2022_Bratislava_shooting&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Reactions"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<section data-mw-section-id="9" id="mwAYM" aria-labelledby="Domestic"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Domestic">Domestic</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=2022_Bratislava_shooting&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Domestic"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwAYQ"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Rainbow_flag_before_the_Grassalkovich_palace.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwAYU"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Rainbow_flag_before_the_Grassalkovich_palace.jpg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/05/Rainbow_flag_before_the_Grassalkovich_palace.jpg/250px-Rainbow_flag_before_the_Grassalkovich_palace.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/05/Rainbow_flag_before_the_Grassalkovich_palace.jpg/500px-Rainbow_flag_before_the_Grassalkovich_palace.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="1000" data-file-height="667" data-file-type="bitmap" height="167" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwAYY"/></a><figcaption id="mwAYc">Rainbow flag raised at the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grassalkovich_Palace" title="Grassalkovich Palace" id="mwAYg">Grassalkovich Palace</a> in solidarity with the LGBT community</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwAYk">The attack was condemned by many political representatives, including mayor of Bratislava <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matúš_Vallo" title="Matúš Vallo" id="mwAYo">Matúš Vallo</a>, and mayor of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Town,_Bratislava" title="Old Town, Bratislava" id="mwAYs">Old Town district</a> Zuzana Aufrichtová.<sup about="#mwt195" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-51" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-51"}}'><a href="#cite_note-51" id="mwAYw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAY0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAY4">[</span>50<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAY8">]</span></span></a></sup> President <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zuzana_Čaputová" title="Zuzana Čaputová" id="mwAZA">Zuzana Čaputová</a> pointed out the likely hateful motive of the act and stated that this hatred towards minorities had been fueled for a long time by "the stupid and irresponsible statements of politicians," whom she called on to stop hateful rhetoric "before more lives are lost".<sup about="#mwt196" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-52" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-52"}}'><a href="#cite_note-52" id="mwAZE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZM">[</span>51<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZQ">]</span></span></a></sup> A <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rainbow_flag_(LGBT)" title="Rainbow flag (LGBT)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAZU">rainbow flag</a> was raised in solidarity with the LGBT community at the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grassalkovich_Palace" title="Grassalkovich Palace" id="mwAZY">Grassalkovich palace</a>.<sup about="#mwt199" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-53" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-53"}}'><a href="#cite_note-53" id="mwAZc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZk">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZo">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAZs"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prime_Minister_of_Slovakia" title="Prime Minister of Slovakia" id="mwAZw">Prime Minister</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eduard_Heger" title="Eduard Heger" id="mwAZ0">Eduard Heger</a> condemned the attack and said "It is unacceptable that anyone should fear for their life because of their way of life..."<sup about="#mwt202" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Heger_7-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Heger"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Heger-7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Heger-7" id="mwAZ4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZ8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaA">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaE">]</span></span></a></sup> He later apologized for describing one's sexual orientation this way and stated support for the LGBT community.</p>

<p id="mwAaI">The rector of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comenius_University" title="Comenius University" id="mwAaM">Comenius University</a> Marek Števček issued a press release informing that a student of the Faculty of Arts, Matúš Horváth, was one of the two victims of the shooting. The rector condemned the killing and called for the entire society to unite against hate and discrimination.<sup about="#mwt205" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-54" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-54"}}'><a href="#cite_note-54" id="mwAaQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAaU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaY">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAac">]</span></span></a></sup> The Faculty of Arts installed the rainbow flag on its building as a sign of its solidarity with the LGBTI community.<sup about="#mwt208" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-55" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-55"}}'><a href="#cite_note-55" id="mwAag"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAak"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAao">[</span>54<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAas">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="10" id="mwAaw" aria-labelledby="International"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="International">International</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=2022_Bratislava_shooting&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: International"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwAa0"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Pietní_akce_a_projev_solidarity_s_lgbtqai+_Slovenskem_2022-10-13,_svíčky_(7299).jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwAa4"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Pietní_akce_a_projev_solidarity_s_lgbtqai+_Slovenskem_2022-10-13,_svíčky_(7299).jpg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/ff/Pietn%C3%AD_akce_a_projev_solidarity_s_lgbtqai%2B_Slovenskem_2022-10-13%2C_sv%C3%AD%C4%8Dky_%287299%29.jpg/250px-Pietn%C3%AD_akce_a_projev_solidarity_s_lgbtqai%2B_Slovenskem_2022-10-13%2C_sv%C3%AD%C4%8Dky_%287299%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/ff/Pietn%C3%AD_akce_a_projev_solidarity_s_lgbtqai%2B_Slovenskem_2022-10-13%2C_sv%C3%AD%C4%8Dky_%287299%29.jpg/500px-Pietn%C3%AD_akce_a_projev_solidarity_s_lgbtqai%2B_Slovenskem_2022-10-13%2C_sv%C3%AD%C4%8Dky_%287299%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="3888" data-file-height="2592" data-file-type="bitmap" height="167" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwAa8"/></a><figcaption id="mwAbA">Pictures of the victims at the memorial event in Brno on October 13, 2022.</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwAbE">Among foreign state and political representatives, Czech Prime Minister <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Petr_Fiala" title="Petr Fiala" id="mwAbI">Petr Fiala</a> expressed his condolences to the survivors of the victims.<sup about="#mwt211" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-56" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-56"}}'><a href="#cite_note-56" id="mwAbM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAbQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbU">[</span>55<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbY">]</span></span></a></sup> The Czech Minister of the Interior, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vít_Rakušan" title="Vít Rakušan" id="mwAbc">Vít Rakušan</a>, added that there is no place in a decent society "for unjustified discrimination of LGBT people".<sup about="#mwt212" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-57" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-57"}}'><a href="#cite_note-57" id="mwAbg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAbk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbo">[</span>56<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbs">]</span></span></a></sup> President of the European Commission <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ursula_von_der_Leyen" title="Ursula von der Leyen" id="mwAbw">Ursula von der Leyen</a> expressed her condolences and added that "We have to protect the LGBTIQ community".<sup about="#mwt215" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-58" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-58"}}'><a href="#cite_note-58" id="mwAb0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAb4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAb8">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt218" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-59" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-59"}}'><a href="#cite_note-59" id="mwAcE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAcI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcM">[</span>58<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
